BC-2.11.021/ADR-044 plan-time-constant-injection contract HELD (NOT amended). Option B (runtime-DataFusion-NOW() relaxation) REJECTED. Rationale: Prism federated execution order (fan-out→fetch→normalize→materialize→DataFusion) — sensor fetch happens BEFORE DataFusion runs; time bound MUST be concrete plan-time Literal::Timestamp to enable ADR-033 time-window push-down (bounded sensor fetch = federation efficiency premise); runtime NOW() evaluated after fetch = too late to bound it. Additional benefits: cross-mode consistency (SQL/filter/pipe), sidesteps PrismQL INTERVAL '7d' vs DataFusion '7 days' parsing ambiguity (emits a literal), pays into day-2 demand-driven caching (detection-rule windows are plan-time bounds). Cost accepted: SQL-mode re-emits folded AST instead of raw string. Implementation: in-flight constant-fold fix-burst (constant-fold inject_now + re-emit SQL-mode/SqlPipe-head + discriminating in/out-of-window temporal tests) implements Option A — no redirect.
